# In exercise of the powers conferred under Bye-law 16 read with Bye-law 18 of the Chartered Accountants Regional Council Bye-laws made under clause (ii) of Regulation 62-H of the Chartered Accountants Regulations, 1949, the Council of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India

NOTIFICATIONS CHARTERED ACCOUNTANTS New Delhi, the 18th December 1954 No. 54-EL(l)/54.—In exercise of the powers conferred under Bye-law 16 read with Bye-law 18 of the Chartered Accountants Regional Council Bye-laws made under clause (ii) of Regulation 62-H of the Chartered Accountants Regulations, 1949, the Council of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India is pleased to direct that the election to the following Regional Councils shall foe held on the 1st April, 1955 at 11 A.M. in the Office of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India, at New Delhi, in the manner prescribed hereunder; —

(i) The Bombay Regional Council;

(ii) The Madras Regional Council;

(iii) The West Bengal Regional Council;

(iv) The Uttar Pradesh Regional Council; and

(v) The Delhi Regional Council.

1. Number of persons to be elected to each Regional Council.—The number of persons to be elected for the different Regional Councils is given below: —

(i) Bombay Regional Council .. .. ,. . . 1 2

(ii) Madras Regional Council .. .. .. ..

(iii) West Bengal Regional Council .. .. ..

(iv) Uttar Pradesh Regional Council ... .. ..

(v) Delhi Regional Council .. .. .. ..

2. Date of election.—The election shall be held on the 1st day of April, 1955 at 11 A.M. in the Office of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India, Mathura Road, New Delhi.

3. Mode of election.—The election shall be made direct by ballot.

4. Members eligible to vote and stand for election—Every Member of the Institute, according to his professional address belonging to the particular region (hereinafter called "the region concerned"), whose name has been borne on the Register of Members maintained by the Council of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India (hereinafter called "the Central Council") as on the 1st October, 1954 and whose name continues to be borne thereon for atleast a period of six months continuously from that date shall be eligible to vote and stand for election to the Regional Council of the region concerned and a list of such Members shall be published in the Gazette of India atleast three months before the date of election. Notwithstanding the fact that a Member's name has been published in the list referred to above, he shall not be eligible either to vote or stand for election, if he has not paid the annual subscription due to the Central and/or Regional Councils or if his name stands removed from the Register of Members maintained by the Central Council on the date of election ( 1345 )

THE GAZETTE OP INDIA EXTRAORDINARY

[PART I

5. Nomination of candidates.-—Every nomination of a candidate shall be in Form 'A' of the Schedule hereto, duly signed by the candidate and by the proposer and seconder, both of whom shall be members of the Institute, whose names are included in the list referred to above and shall be sent by Registered Post by name to Shri S. Venkataraman, Secretary, the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India, Delhi—Mathura Road, Post Box No. 268, New Delhi-1, so as to reach him before 5 P.M. on the 18th January, 1955.

6. Fees.—Every candidate standing for election shall pay a fee of Rs. 50 (Rupees Fifty only) by Demand Draft drawn in favour of the Secretary, the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India (by designation), payable at New Delhi, which shall be submitted along with the nomination paper in Form 'A* referred to above.

7. Withdrawal of candidature.—Any candidate may withdraw his candidature by notice in writing signed by him and" delivered to the said Shri S. Venkataraman before 3 P.M. on the 25th January, 1955. No person who has thus withdrawn his candidature shall be allowed io cancel his withdrawal. The withdrawal of candidature shall be intimated by the Secretary of the Central Council to the other candidates standing for election from the region concerned. Any candidate who withdraws his candidature under this rule shall be entitled on an application made by him in writing in this behalf to the refund of half the amount of fee deposited by him under Rule 6 above.

8. For the purposes of this election, the provisions of Chapter V of the Chartered Accountants Regulations, 1949, shall apply unless they are inconsistent with the provisions contained herein and in the Chartered Accountants Regional Council Bye-laws, in which case, the latter shall prevail.

SCHEDULE FORM 'A' Form of nomination of a candidate, for election to the Regional Council for the Region We, the undersigned Members of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India, belonging to me Region, being qualified to vote in the election of members to the Regional Council of the Region, dohereby nominate Shri who is a Member of the Institute and whose name appears in the list of members eligible to vote in the said election, as a candidate for the said election to be held on the 1st day of April, 1955.
